. eststo clear

. forvalues i = 1/5 {
  2.         eststo: reghdfe y `x', absorb(\$s`i') vce($se)
  3.                 * Add indicators for baseline 
.                 estadd local timeFE     "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  4.                 estadd local censusPA   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  5.                 * Add sample sizes report
.                 mat femat = e(dof_table)
  6.                 local days = femat[1,1]
  7.                 estadd local days "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `days' $}"              
  8.                 local clusters: display %9.0fc `e(N_clust1)'
  9.                 estadd local clusters "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `clusters' $}"              
 10.                 local nobs: display %9.0fc `e(N)'
 11.                 estadd local nobs = "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `nobs' $}"    
 12.                 * Add indicators for additional controls
.                 if `i'==2 {
 13.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 14.                 }
 15.                 else if `i'==3 {
 16.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 17.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 18.                 }
 19.                 else if `i'==4 {
 20.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 21.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 22.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 23.                 }
 24.                 else if `i'==5 {
 25.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 26.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 27.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 28.                         estadd local POI                "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 29.                 }
 30. }

      name:  _log

. local x mismatch_race

. 
. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. * Estimate specifications
. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. eststo clear

. forvalues i = 1/5 {
  2.         eststo: reghdfe y c.`x'##i.ncentral_d, absorb(\$s`i') vce($se)
  3.                 * Add indicators for baseline 
.                 estadd local timeFE     "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  4.                 estadd local censusPA   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  5.                 * Add sample sizes report
.                 mat femat = e(dof_table)
  6.                 local days = femat[1,1]
  7.                 estadd local days "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `days' $}"              
  8.                 local clusters: display %9.0fc `e(N_clust1)'
  9.                 estadd local clusters "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `clusters' $}"              
 10.                 local nobs: display %9.0fc `e(N)'
 11.                 estadd local nobs = "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `nobs' $}"    
 12.                 * Test mismatch + mismatch*ncentral = 0 [total effect outside central]
.                 qui test `x' + `x'#1.ncentral_d = 0
 13.                 estadd local pval = string(round(`r(p)', 0.001))
 14.                 * Add indicators for additional controls
.                 if `i'==2 {
 15.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 16.                 }
 17.                 else if `i'==3 {
 18.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 19.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 20.                 }
 21.                 else if `i'==4 {
 22.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 23.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 24.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 25.                 }
 26.                 else if `i'==5 {
 27.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 28.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 29.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 30.                         estadd local POI                "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 31.                 }
 32. }

. local x1 mismatch_p75_p2r

. // local x2 mismatch_p70_r2p

. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. * Estimate specifications
. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. eststo clear

. forvalues i = 1/5 {
  2.         eststo: reghdfe y i.`x1' i.`x2', absorb(\$s`i') vce($se)
  3.                 * Add indicators for baseline 
.                 estadd local timeFE     "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  4.                 estadd local censusPA   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  5.                 * Add sample sizes report
.                 mat femat = e(dof_table)
  6.                 local days = femat[1,1]
  7.                 estadd local days "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `days' $}"              
  8.                 local clusters: display %9.0fc `e(N_clust1)'
  9.                 estadd local clusters "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `clusters' $}"              
 10.                 local nobs: display %9.0fc `e(N)'
 11.                 estadd local nobs = "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `nobs' $}"    
 12.                 * Test r2p + p2r = 0
.                 qui test 1.`x1' + 1.`x2' = 0
 13.                 estadd local pval = string(round(`r(p)', 0.001))
 14.                 * Add indicators for additional controls
.                 if `i'==2 {
 15.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 16.                 }
 17.                 else if `i'==3 {
 18.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 19.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 20.                 }
 21.                 else if `i'==4 {
 22.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 23.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 24.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 25.                 }
 26.                 else if `i'==5 {
 27.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 28.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 29.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 30.                         estadd local POI                "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 31.                 }
 32. }

. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. * Estimate specifications
. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. eststo clear

. forvalues i = 1/5 {
  2.         eststo: reghdfe y i.`x'##i.ncentral_d, absorb(\$s`i') vce($se)
  3.                 * Add indicators for baseline 
.                 estadd local timeFE     "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  4.                 estadd local censusPA   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  5.                 * Add sample sizes report
.                 mat femat = e(dof_table)
  6.                 local days = femat[1,1]
  7.                 estadd local days "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `days' $}"              
  8.                 local clusters: display %9.0fc `e(N_clust1)'
  9.                 estadd local clusters "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `clusters' $}"              
 10.                 local nobs: display %9.0fc `e(N)'
 11.                 estadd local nobs = "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `nobs' $}"    
 12.                 * Test mismatch + mismatch*ncentral = 0 [total effect outside central]
.                 qui test 1.`x' + 1.`x'#1.ncentral_d = 0
 13.                 estadd local pval = string(round(`r(p)', 0.001))
 14.                 * Add indicators for additional controls
.                 if `i'==2 {
 15.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 16.                 }
 17.                 else if `i'==3 {
 18.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 19.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 20.                 }
 21.                 else if `i'==4 {
 22.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 23.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 24.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 25.                 }
 26.                 else if `i'==5 {
 27.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 28.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 29.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 30.                         estadd local POI                "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 31.                 }
 32. }

. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. * Estimate specifications
. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. eststo clear

. forvalues i = 1/5 {
  2.         eststo: reghdfe y i.`x'##i.ncentral_d, absorb(\$s`i') vce($se)
  3.                 * Add indicators for baseline 
.                 estadd local timeFE     "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  4.                 estadd local censusPA   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  5.                 * Add sample sizes report
.                 mat femat = e(dof_table)
  6.                 local days = femat[1,1]
  7.                 estadd local days "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `days' $}"              
  8.                 local clusters: display %9.0fc `e(N_clust1)'
  9.                 estadd local clusters "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `clusters' $}"              
 10.                 local nobs: display %9.0fc `e(N)'
 11.                 estadd local nobs = "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `nobs' $}"    
 12.                 * Test mismatch + mismatch*ncentral = 0 [total effect outside central]
.                 qui test 1.`x' + 1.`x'#1.ncentral_d = 0
 13.                 estadd local pval = string(round(`r(p)', 0.001))
 14.                 * Add indicators for additional controls
.                 if `i'==2 {
 15.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 16.                 }
 17.                 else if `i'==3 {
 18.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 19.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 20.                 }
 21.                 else if `i'==4 {
 22.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 23.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 24.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 25.                 }
 26.                 else if `i'==5 {
 27.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 28.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 29.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 30.                         estadd local POI                "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 31.                 }
 32. }

. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. * Estimate specifications

. eststo clear

. forvalues i = 1/5 {
  2.         eststo: reghdfe y i.`x'##i.ncentral_d, absorb(\$s`i') vce($se)
  3.                 * Add indicators for baseline 
.                 estadd local timeFE     "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  4.                 estadd local censusPA   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  5.                 * Add sample sizes report
.                 mat femat = e(dof_table)
  6.                 local days = femat[1,1]
  7.                 estadd local days "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `days' $}"              
  8.                 local clusters: display %9.0fc `e(N_clust1)'
  9.                 estadd local clusters "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `clusters' $}"              
 10.                 local nobs: display %9.0fc `e(N)'
 11.                 estadd local nobs = "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `nobs' $}"    
 12.                 * Test mismatch + mismatch*ncentral = 0 [total effect outside central]
.                 qui test 1.`x' + 1.`x'#1.ncentral_d = 0
 13.                 estadd local pval = string(round(`r(p)', 0.001))
 14.                 * Add indicators for additional controls
.                 if `i'==2 {
 15.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 16.                 }
 17.                 else if `i'==3 {
 18.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 19.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 20.                 }
 21.                 else if `i'==4 {
 22.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 23.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 24.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 25.                 }
 26.                 else if `i'==5 {
 27.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 28.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 29.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 30.                         estadd local POI                "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 31.                 }
 32. }

. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. * Estimate specifications
. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. eststo clear

. forvalues i = 1/5 {
  2.         eststo: reghdfe y `x', absorb(\$s`i') vce($se)
  3.                 * Add indicators for baseline 
.                 estadd local timeFE     "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  4.                 estadd local censusPA   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  5.                 * Add sample sizes report
.                 mat femat = e(dof_table)
  6.                 local days = femat[1,1]
  7.                 estadd local days "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `days' $}"              
  8.                 local clusters: display %9.0fc `e(N_clust1)'
  9.                 estadd local clusters "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `clusters' $}"              
 10.                 local nobs: display %9.0fc `e(N)'
 11.                 estadd local nobs = "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `nobs' $}"    
 12.                 * Add indicators for additional controls
.                 if `i'==2 {
 13.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"

. eststo clear

. forvalues i = 1/5 {
  2.         eststo: reghdfe y i.`x1' i.`x2', absorb(\$s`i') vce($se)
  3.                 * Add indicators for baseline 
.                 estadd local timeFE     "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  4.                 estadd local censusPA   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  5.                 * Add sample sizes report
.                 mat femat = e(dof_table)
  6.                 local days = femat[1,1]
  7.                 estadd local days "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `days' $}"              
  8.                 local clusters: display %9.0fc `e(N_clust1)'
  9.                 estadd local clusters "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `clusters' $}"              
 10.                 local nobs: display %9.0fc `e(N)'
 11.                 estadd local nobs = "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `nobs' $}"    
 12.                 * Test r2p + p2r = 0
.                 qui test 1.`x1' + 1.`x2' = 0
 13.                 estadd local pval = string(round(`r(p)', 0.001))
 14.                 * Add indicators for additional controls
.                 if `i'==2 {
 15.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 16.                 }
 17.                 else if `i'==3 {
 18.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 19.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 20.                 }
 21.                 else if `i'==4 {

 24.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 25.                 }
 26.                 else if `i'==5 {
 27.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 28.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"

. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. * Estimate specifications
. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. eststo clear

. forvalues i = 1/5 {
  2.         eststo: reghdfe y i.`x'##i.ncentral_d, absorb(\$s`i') vce($se)
  3.                 * Add indicators for baseline 
.                 estadd local timeFE     "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  4.                 estadd local censusPA   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  5.                 * Add sample sizes report
.                 mat femat = e(dof_table)
  6.                 local days = femat[1,1]
  7.                 estadd local days "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `days' $}"              
  8.                 local clusters: display %9.0fc `e(N_clust1)'
  9.                 estadd local clusters "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `clusters' $}"              
 10.                 local nobs: display %9.0fc `e(N)'
 11.                 estadd local nobs = "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `nobs' $}"    
 12.                 * Test mismatch + mismatch*ncentral = 0 [total effect outside central]
.                 qui test 1.`x' + 1.`x'#1.ncentral_d = 0
 13.                 estadd local pval = string(round(`r(p)', 0.001))
 14.                 * Add indicators for additional controls
.                 if `i'==2 {
 15.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 16.                 }
 17.                 else if `i'==3 {
 18.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 19.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 20.                 }
 21.                 else if `i'==4 {
 22.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 23.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 24.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 25.                 }
 26.                 else if `i'==5 {
 27.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 28.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 29.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 30.                         estadd local POI                "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 31.                 }
 32. }

. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. * Estimate specifications
. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. eststo clear

. forvalues i = 1/5 {
  2.         eststo: reghdfe y i.`x1' i.`x2', absorb(\$s`i') vce($se)
  3.                 * Add indicators for baseline 
.                 estadd local timeFE     "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  4.                 estadd local censusPA   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  5.                 * Add sample sizes report
.                 mat femat = e(dof_table)
  6.                 local days = femat[1,1]
  7.                 estadd local days "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `days' $}"              
  8.                 local clusters: display %9.0fc `e(N_clust1)'
  9.                 estadd local clusters "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `clusters' $}"              
 10.                 local nobs: display %9.0fc `e(N)'
 11.                 estadd local nobs = "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `nobs' $}"    
 12.                 * Test r2p + p2r = 0
.                 qui test 1.`x1' + 1.`x2' = 0
 13.                 estadd local pval = string(round(`r(p)', 0.001))
 14.                 * Add indicators for additional controls
.                 if `i'==2 {
 15.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 16.                 }
 17.                 else if `i'==3 {
 18.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 19.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 20.                 }
 21.                 else if `i'==4 {
 22.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 23.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 24.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 25.                 }
 26.                 else if `i'==5 {
 27.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 28.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 29.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 30.                         estadd local POI                "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 31.                 }
 32. }

. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. * Estimate specifications
. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. eststo clear

. forvalues i = 1/5 {
  2.         eststo: reghdfe y i.`x'##i.ncentral_d, absorb(\$s`i') vce($se)
  3.                 * Add indicators for baseline 
.                 estadd local timeFE     "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  4.                 estadd local censusPA   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  5.                 * Add sample sizes report
.                 mat femat = e(dof_table)
  6.                 local days = femat[1,1]
  7.                 estadd local days "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `days' $}"              
  8.                 local clusters: display %9.0fc `e(N_clust1)'
  9.                 estadd local clusters "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `clusters' $}"              
 10.                 local nobs: display %9.0fc `e(N)'
 11.                 estadd local nobs = "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `nobs' $}"    
 12.                 * Test mismatch + mismatch*ncentral = 0 [total effect outside central]
.                 qui test 1.`x' + 1.`x'#1.ncentral_d = 0
 13.                 estadd local pval = string(round(`r(p)', 0.001))
 14.                 * Add indicators for additional controls
.                 if `i'==2 {
 15.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 16.                 }
 17.                 else if `i'==3 {
 18.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 19.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 20.                 }
 21.                 else if `i'==4 {
 22.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 23.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 24.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 25.                 }
 26.                 else if `i'==5 {
 27.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 28.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 29.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 30.                         estadd local POI                "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 31.                 }
 32. }

. forvalues i = 1/5 {
  2.         eststo: reghdfe y `x1' `x2', absorb(\$s`i') vce($se)
  3.                 * Add indicators for baseline 
.                 estadd local timeFE     "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  4.                 estadd local censusPA   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  5.                 * Add sample sizes report
.                 mat femat = e(dof_table)
  6.                 local days = femat[1,1]
  7.                 estadd local days "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `days' $}"              
  8.                 local clusters: display %9.0fc `e(N_clust1)'
  9.                 estadd local clusters "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `clusters' $}"              
 10.                 local nobs: display %9.0fc `e(N)'
 11.                 estadd local nobs = "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `nobs' $}"    
 12.                 * Test r2p + p2r = 0
.                 qui test `x1' + `x2' = 0
 13.                 estadd local pval = string(round(`r(p)', 0.001))
 14.                 * Add indicators for additional controls
.                 if `i'==2 {
 15.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 16.                 }
 17.                 else if `i'==3 {
 18.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 19.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 20.                 }
 21.                 else if `i'==4 {
 22.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 23.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 24.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 25.                 }
 26.                 else if `i'==5 {
 27.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 28.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 29.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 30.                         estadd local POI                "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 31.                 }
 32. }

. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. * Estimate specifications

. eststo clear

. forvalues i = 1/5 {
  2.         eststo: reghdfe y c.`x'##i.ncentral_d, absorb(\$s`i') vce($se)
  3.                 * Add indicators for baseline 
.                 estadd local timeFE     "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  4.                 estadd local censusPA   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  5.                 * Add sample sizes report
.                 mat femat = e(dof_table)
  6.                 local days = femat[1,1]
  7.                 estadd local days "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `days' $}"              
  8.                 local clusters: display %9.0fc `e(N_clust1)'
  9.                 estadd local clusters "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `clusters' $}"              
 10.                 local nobs: display %9.0fc `e(N)'
 11.                 estadd local nobs = "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `nobs' $}"    
 12.                 * Test mismatch + mismatch*ncentral = 0 [total effect outside central]
.                 qui test `x' + `x'#1.ncentral_d = 0
 13.                 estadd local pval = string(round(`r(p)', 0.001))
 14.                 * Add indicators for additional controls
.                 if `i'==2 {
 15.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 16.                 }
 17.                 else if `i'==3 {
 18.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 19.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 20.                 }
 21.                 else if `i'==4 {
 22.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 23.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 24.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 25.                 }
 26.                 else if `i'==5 {
 27.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 28.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 29.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 30.                         estadd local POI                "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 31.                 }
 32. }

. local x mismatch_p75

. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. * Estimate specifications
. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. eststo clear

. forvalues i = 1/5 {
  2.         eststo: reghdfe y i.`x'##i.ncentral_d, absorb(\$s`i') vce($se)
  3.                 * Add indicators for baseline 
.                 estadd local timeFE     "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  4.                 estadd local censusPA   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  5.                 * Add sample sizes report
.                 mat femat = e(dof_table)
  6.                 local days = femat[1,1]
  7.                 estadd local days "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `days' $}"              
  8.                 local clusters: display %9.0fc `e(N_clust1)'
  9.                 estadd local clusters "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `clusters' $}"              
 10.                 local nobs: display %9.0fc `e(N)'
 11.                 estadd local nobs = "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `nobs' $}"    
 12.                 * Test mismatch + mismatch*ncentral = 0 [total effect outside central]
.                 qui test 1.`x' + 1.`x'#1.ncentral_d = 0
 13.                 estadd local pval = string(round(`r(p)', 0.001))
 14.                 * Add indicators for additional controls
.                 if `i'==2 {
 15.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 16.                 }
 17.                 else if `i'==3 {
 18.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 19.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 20.                 }
 21.                 else if `i'==4 {
 22.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 23.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 24.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 25.                 }
 26.                 else if `i'==5 {
 27.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 28.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 29.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 30.                         estadd local POI                "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 31.                 }
 32. }

  2.         eststo: reghdfe y `x' if `condition', absorb(\$s`i') vce($se)
  3.                 * Add indicators for baseline 
.                 estadd local timeFE     "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  4.                 estadd local censusPA   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  5.                 * Add sample sizes report
.                 mat femat = e(dof_table)
  6.                 local days = femat[1,1]
  7.                 estadd local days "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `days' $}"              
  8.                 local clusters: display %9.0fc `e(N_clust1)'
  9.                 estadd local clusters "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `clusters' $}"              
 10.                 local nobs: display %9.0fc `e(N)'
 11.                 estadd local nobs = "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `nobs' $}"    
 12.                 * Add indicators for additional controls
.                 if `i'==2 {
 13.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"

. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. * Estimate specifications
. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. eststo clear

. forvalues i = 1/5 {
  2.         eststo: reghdfe y i.`x1' i.`x2' if `condition', absorb(\$s`i') vce($se)
  3.                 * Add indicators for baseline 
.                 estadd local timeFE     "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  4.                 estadd local censusPA   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  5.                 * Add sample sizes report
.                 mat femat = e(dof_table)
  6.                 local days = femat[1,1]
  7.                 estadd local days "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `days' $}"              
  8.                 local clusters: display %9.0fc `e(N_clust1)'
  9.                 estadd local clusters "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `clusters' $}"              
 10.                 local nobs: display %9.0fc `e(N)'
 11.                 estadd local nobs = "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `nobs' $}"    
 12.                 * Test r2p + p2r = 0
.                 qui test 1.`x1' + 1.`x2' = 0
 13.                 estadd local pval = string(round(`r(p)', 0.001))
 14.                 * Add indicators for additional controls
.                 if `i'==2 {
 15.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 16.                 }
 17.                 else if `i'==3 {
 18.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 19.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 20.                 }
 21.                 else if `i'==4 {
 22.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 23.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 24.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 25.                 }
 26.                 else if `i'==5 {
 27.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 28.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 29.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 30.                         estadd local POI                "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 31.                 }
 32. }

. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. * Estimate specifications
. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. eststo clear

. forvalues i = 1/5 {
  2.         eststo: reghdfe y i.`x'##i.ncentral_d if `condition', absorb(\$s`i') vce($se)
  3.                 * Add indicators for baseline 
.                 estadd local timeFE     "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  4.                 estadd local censusPA   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  5.                 * Add sample sizes report
.                 mat femat = e(dof_table)
  6.                 local days = femat[1,1]
  7.                 estadd local days "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `days' $}"              
  8.                 local clusters: display %9.0fc `e(N_clust1)'
  9.                 estadd local clusters "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `clusters' $}"              
 10.                 local nobs: display %9.0fc `e(N)'
 11.                 estadd local nobs = "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `nobs' $}"    
 12.                 * Test mismatch + mismatch*ncentral = 0 [total effect outside central]
.                 qui test 1.`x' + 1.`x'#1.ncentral_d = 0
 13.                 estadd local pval = string(round(`r(p)', 0.001))
 14.                 * Add indicators for additional controls
.                 if `i'==2 {
 15.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 16.                 }
 17.                 else if `i'==3 {
 18.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 19.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 20.                 }
 21.                 else if `i'==4 {
 22.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 23.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 24.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 25.                 }
 26.                 else if `i'==5 {
 27.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 28.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 29.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 30.                         estadd local POI                "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 31.                 }
 32. }

  8.                 local clusters: display %9.0fc `e(N_clust1)'
  9.                 estadd local clusters "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `clusters' $}"              
 10.                 local nobs: display %9.0fc `e(N)'

.                 if `i'==2 {

. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. * Estimate specifications
. *~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
. eststo clear

. forvalues i = 1/5 {
  2.         eststo: reghdfe y `x1' `x2' if `condition', absorb(\$s`i') vce($se)
  3.                 * Add indicators for baseline 
.                 estadd local timeFE     "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  4.                 estadd local censusPA   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"
  5.                 * Add sample sizes report
.                 mat femat = e(dof_table)
  6.                 local days = femat[1,1]
  7.                 estadd local days "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `days' $}"              
  8.                 local clusters: display %9.0fc `e(N_clust1)'
  9.                 estadd local clusters "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `clusters' $}"              
 10.                 local nobs: display %9.0fc `e(N)'
 11.                 estadd local nobs = "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `nobs' $}"    
 12.                 * Test r2p + p2r = 0
.                 qui test `x1' + `x2' = 0
 13.                 estadd local pval = string(round(`r(p)', 0.001))
 14.                 * Add indicators for additional controls
.                 if `i'==2 {
 15.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 16.                 }
 17.                 else if `i'==3 {
 18.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 19.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 20.                 }
 21.                 else if `i'==4 {
 22.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 23.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 24.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 25.                 }
 26.                 else if `i'==5 {
 27.                         estadd local demo               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"        
 28.                         estadd local business   "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                                
 29.                         estadd local rain               "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 30.                         estadd local POI                "\multicolumn{1}{c}{\checkmark}"                        
 31.                 }

  9.                 estadd local clusters "\multicolumn{1}{c}{$ `clusters' $}"

 12.                 * Test mismatch + mismatch*ncentral = 0 [total effect outside central]
.                 qui test `x' + `x'#1.ncentral_d = 0
 13.                 estadd local pval = string(round(`r(p)', 0.001))

.                 if `i'==2 {

. #delimit;

. coefplot (est1, msymbol(O)  msize(`MSIZE') mlwidth($MLWIDTH) )
>                  (est2, msymbol(Oh) msize(`MSIZE') mlwidth($MLWIDTH) offset())
>                  (est3, msymbol(dh) msize(`MSIZE') mlwidth($MLWIDTH) offset())
